# Builtins porting inventory

## Current section

Native intrinsic signatures and Darklang bridges supply the compiler callable surface. Literal upstream function/value registration candidates are individually generated under file scopes; computed registration constructors remain explicit gaps rather than an inferred registry count.

## Intended section

- source: independent-implementation
- behavior: identical
- freshness: follow-upstream
- destination: Same upstream public contracts, with native or Darklang implementations and separate compiler-private support.
- disposition: required

## Open questions

- Export the assembled registries, signatures, aliases, and visibility before claiming API completeness.

## Registration discovery

714 literal function/value registration candidates; 7 computed-name/version constructors remain explicit discovery gaps.
These are not assembled registry counts. Constructors can be invoked repeatedly, renamed, filtered, or combined by host-specific registries.
File policies cover all their declarations; nested scopes use `FILE/declarations/fn/NAME` or `FILE/declarations/value/NAME`.
Same-spelling compiler declarations are only candidates, not proven bindings, compatible signatures, or working implementations.
